  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙引力辐射为探索标准模型之外的物理提供了重要窗口，但粒子衰变产生引力波的频谱行为（特别是低频行为）尚未被充分理解，需要系统研究以评估其探测前景。

Method: 采用两种独立但等价的方法计算衰减产物产生的引力波能谱dE/dω：(1) 利用Weinberg软引力子定理；(2) 直接求解运动方程。并将Weinberg软引力子定理与引力记忆公式的关联推广到包含末态散射效应，最终计算宇宙学随机引力波能谱h²Ω_h。

Result: 发现闵可夫斯基时空中能谱在特征截止频率以上呈平坦的韧致辐射/记忆谱，以下呈二次方抑制标度；宇宙学随机引力波能谱在截止频率以上呈线性标度，以下呈三次方标度。低频信号相对朴素线性外推被抑制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了早期宇宙粒子衰变引力波低频信号的抑制效应及其对探测前景的影响，并进一步建立了韧致辐射与记忆效应在宇宙学随机引力波谱背景下的联系。

Abstract: Gravitational radiation from the early Universe offers a powerful window into physics beyond the Standard Model (SM). In this paper, we investigate the low-frequency tail of gravitational radiation produced by the decay of massive particles. The energy spectrum in Minkowski space-time, $dE/dω$, exhibits two distinct regimes: a flat bremsstrahlung/memory spectrum above a characteristic cutoff frequency and a suppressed quadratic frequency scaling below it. The typical rate of momentum change of the decay products determines the cutoff frequency. We calculate $dE/dω$ in two independent but equivalent ways: using Weinberg's soft graviton theorem and by solving the equations of motion. This further establishes the connection between Weinberg's soft graviton theorem and the gravitational memory formula, which we generalize to incorporate the effects of final-state scattering. We then compute the cosmological stochastic gravitational energy spectrum $h^2Ω_{\rm h}$ generated by many particle decays in the early Universe. We demonstrate that $h^2Ω_{\rm h}$ exhibits linear scaling with frequency above the cutoff frequency, transitioning to cubic scaling below it. Our findings reveal a suppression of the low-frequency signal compared to a naive linear extrapolation, with implications for detection prospects. Our results also reveal the connection between bremsstrahlung and memory in the context of cosmological stochastic gravitational energy spectra from the early Universe.

</details>


### [97] [Resonant Induced Orbital Electron Capture: Novel method for low-energy $\barν_e$ detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25001)
*Evgeny Akhmedov,Thierry Lasserre,Leonardo Maturi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于轨道电子诱导俘获的新型低能电子反中微子探测方法，利用共振条件实现对能量低于1.8 MeV（逆β衰变阈值）的反中微子的探测，并给出候选核素及符合探测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反中微子探测依赖于逆β衰变，其能量阈值为1.8 MeV，导致更低能量的反中微子（如部分反应堆中微子、地球中微子、keV尺度热太阳中微子）从未被探测到。作者旨在突破这一能量限制，开辟探测此前从未触及的低能中微子的新途径。

Method: 该方法基于轨道电子被原子核诱导俘获过程，该过程具有共振特性：反中微子能量必须精确匹配最终与初始原子系统间的能量差。对于连续谱源，共振条件无需精细调谐即可满足，有效截面取决于反中微子通量在共振能量处的谱强度而非中微子能量本身。作者识别了多个候选核素，可实现到子核激发态的跃迁，对应共振能量低于1.8 MeV，并提出原子-核符合信号用于本底抑制。

Result: 识别出多个可实现共振反中微子能量低于逆β衰变阈值的候选核素，并提出了这种新方法在低能反应堆中微子、地球中微子以及keV尺度热太阳中微子探测中的应用前景，同时讨论了在中微子振荡实验和反应堆监测中的潜在应用。

Conclusion: 该方法为低能反中微子探测提供了一条全新途径，突破了传统1.8 MeV能量阈值限制，有望首次探测此前无法观测的低能中微子信号，并为中微子物理学和反应堆监测等领域提供新工具。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to detecting low-energy electron antineutrinos based on the induced capture of orbital electrons by nuclei. This process is resonant, requiring the antineutrino energy to precisely match the energy difference between the final and initial atomic systems. For continuous-spectrum sources, the resonance conditions can be satisfied without fine-tuning, and the effective cross sections depend on the spectral intensity of the $\barν_e$ flux at the resonance rather than on the neutrino energy itself. This opens the possibility of detecting neutrinos of never previously probed low energies. We identify a number of candidate nuclides that allow transitions to excited states of the daughter nuclei corresponding to resonant $\barν_e$ energies below the inverse $β$ decay threshold of 1.8 MeV, and we consider a distinctive atomic-nuclear coincidence signature for background rejection. We discuss implications of the proposed method for detecting low-energy reactor neutrinos, geoneutrinos, and keV-scale thermal solar neutrinos. Applications to neutrino oscillation experiments and to reactor monitoring are also briefly discussed.

</details>


### [98] [NuGlass: A Fast and Interactive 3D Visualization of Neutrino Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25049)
*Chao Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍NuGlass，一个开源的、完全在客户端运行的Web应用程序，提供六种互补的全交互3D视图来可视化三味中微子振荡现象，包括振荡图表面、双概率轨迹、Bloch球投影、物质本征态相量分解、味组成管和世界线视图，可作为研究工具和公众科普展品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡现象学通常通过二维投影图（概率曲线、振荡图和双概率图）来传达，但这些图将多参数函数（基线、能量、物质密度、质量排序和CP破坏相位）压平为单一静态图形，无法全面展示三味演化的丰富信息，存在可视化维度不足的问题。

Method: NuGlass渲染六个互补的、完全交互的3D视图来展示相同的三味演化：1)振荡图表面；2)沿能量方向挤压的双概率轨迹；3)量子味态的Bloch球投影；4)振荡振幅的物质本征态相量分解；5)沿传播基线的味组成管；6)以L/E绘制概率、显示物质诱导的真空简并破缺的世界线视图。此外还提供振荡通道切换器、实验预设和可定制的动画控制。

Result: NuGlass既可作为研究探索工具，也可作为公众参与展品使用。应用完全在客户端运行，已部署于https://czczc.github.io/nuglass/。

Conclusion: NuGlass通过多视图3D交互可视化的方式，弥补了传统二维投影图在呈现中微子振荡多维参数空间方面的不足，为研究界和公众提供了更直观、更全面的中微子振荡理解工具。

Abstract: Neutrino oscillation phenomenology is usually communicated through two-dimensional projections (probability curves, oscillograms, and bi-probability plots), each of which flattens a function of many parameters (baseline, energy, matter density, mass ordering, and the CP-violating phase) onto a single static figure. We present NuGlass, an open-source, web-based application that renders six complementary, fully interactive 3D views of the same underlying three-flavor evolution: an oscillogram surface, a bi-probability trajectory extruded along energy, a Bloch-sphere projection of the quantum flavor state, a matter-eigenstate phasor decomposition of the oscillation amplitude, a flavor-composition tube along the propagation baseline, and a worldline view that plots the probabilities against L/E and makes the matter-induced breaking of the vacuum degeneracy visible. An oscillation channel switcher, experiment presets, and customizable animation controls are provided. NuGlass can be used both as a research exploration tool and as a public-engagement exhibit. The application runs entirely client-side and is deployed at https://czczc.github.io/nuglass/ .

</details>


### [99] [Energy-independent neutrino dephasing from an ultralight axion-like background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25069)
*B. A. Couto e Silva,B. L. Sánchez-Vega*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究极轻轴子（ALP）暗物质背景下的中微子振荡。通过自底向上的导数相互作用，发现对角耦合下主导相位由ALP场在生产与探测端之差决定；未解析的背景会通过端点场差的特征函数乘以每个干涉项。对固定振幅相干模且相位均匀采样时，因子为J0(Aij)。在慢飞行和小去相区域，该结果与高斯维里化晕处理在二阶一致，产生主导的L²E⁰抑制。论文识别了有限曝光和相干条件，并利用JUNO首次数据将能量无关阻尼约束映射到味参数g2=g3的基准上，给出|Δg21|=|Δg31|≲0.90 GeV⁻¹（局域ALP密度0.4 GeV/cm³）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极轻轴子暗物质如何影响中微子振荡，特别是未解析（unresolved）暗物质背景带来的统计效应，以及不同统计描述（相干模与维里化晕）之间的适用条件与联系，从而为实验数据提供理论解读。

Method: 采用自底向上的有效场论（EFT）方法，引入活性中微子与极轻ALP的导数相互作用。在质量基上考虑对角耦合，计算生产与探测端ALP场差所贡献的相对相位。通过特征函数方法处理未解析背景对干涉项的调制，推导固定振幅相干模下的J0因子，并与高斯维里化晕处理进行对比，在慢飞行和小去相区域做级数展开至二阶。最后将JUNO数据的能量无关阻尼约束映射到味基准耦合参数。

Result: 对于固定振幅相干模、相位均匀采样，干涉项被乘以J0(Aij)；在慢飞行和小去相极限下，与维里化晕处理在二阶一致，给出L²E⁰的主导抑制。将JUNO首次数据的阻尼约束映射到g2=g3味基准，在局域ALP密度0.4 GeV/cm³下得到|Δg21|=|Δg31|≲0.90 GeV⁻¹的低质量平台约束。

Conclusion: 建立了相干模与维里化晕两种统计描述的统一框架，明确了各自的适用条件，并将实验约束转化为模型无关的EFT基准数值，为未来中微子实验探测极轻轴子暗物质提供了理论参考。

Abstract: We study neutrino oscillations in an ultralight axion-like particle (ALP) dark-matter background using a bottom-up derivative interaction for active neutrinos. For couplings diagonal in the neutrino mass basis, the leading relative phase is fixed by the difference of the ALP field between production and detection. More generally, an unresolved background multiplies each interference term by the characteristic function of this endpoint field difference. For a single fixed-amplitude coherent mode with uniformly sampled phase, the factor is $J_0(A_{ij})$. In the slow-flight and small-dephasing regimes, this result and a Gaussian virialized-halo treatment agree at quadratic order and produce a leading $L^2E^0$ suppression. We identify the finite-exposure and coherence conditions under which the two statistical descriptions apply. As an application, we analytically map the energy-independent damping constraint derived from the first JUNO data onto the flavor benchmark $g_2=g_3$. For a local ALP density of $0.4\mathrm{GeV}/\mathrm{cm}^3$, the low-mass plateau gives $|Δg_{21}|=|Δg_{31}|\lesssim0.90\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. This number is a baseline-matched EFT recast of the reference damping analysis. No specific ultraviolet completion is assumed.

</details>


### [100] [Impact of local and global event conditions in prompt charm hadron ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25086)
*Luis Carlos Cruz Peralta,Lizardo Valencia Palomo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用Pythia 8.312实现了SC-CR和BLC-CR+Ropes两种碎裂模型，检验其对LHC能区质子-质子碰撞中粲强子比值的描述能力，并研究了喷注存在和相对横向活动度对强子化过程的影响。结果表明两种模型均能很好地再现ALICE在√s=13 TeV的不同事件条件下的数据，且重子-介子比率对底层事件活动存在依赖性，暗示重子和介子的强子化受局部效应或全局事件条件的影响不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电子-质子与电子-正电子碰撞中提取的碎裂函数无法解释LHC能区质子-质子碰撞中的瞬发粲重子比值，因此需要开发新模型和事件生成器调优来正确描述ALICE实验数据。

Method: 使用Pythia 8.312实现SC-CR（弦色重组）和BLC-CR+Ropes（玻色子-轻夸克色重组加弦绳模型）两种模型，对√s=13 TeV下不同事件条件以及喷注内/外和不同相对横向活动度范围中的粲强子比值进行系统性研究。

Result: 两种模型均能较好地再现Λc+/D0、Σc/D0、D+/D0和D*+/D0比值数据；当瞬发粲强子位于喷注内时，所有比值相对无喷注要求时均有修正；仅重子-介子比值对底层事件活动表现出依赖性。

Conclusion: 重子和介子的强子化过程在局部效应（如喷注环境）或全局事件条件（如底层事件活动）的影响下表现出不同的响应，说明二者的碎裂机制并非以相同方式受环境调节。

Abstract: Fragmentation functions extracted from electron-proton and electron-positron collisions have shown not to be able to explain prompt charm hadron ratios in proton-proton collisions at the LHC energies. New models and event generator tunes have been developed to correctly describe the data from the ALICE experiment in proton-proton collisions at different center of mass energies. In this work Pythia 8.312 is used to implement two of those models: the SC-CR and BLC-CR + Ropes. Both show a good performance to reproduce the data at \sqrt{s}} = 13 TeV in different event conditions for the $Λ_{c}^{+}/\rm{D}^0$, $Σ_{c}^{0,+,++}/\rm{D}^0$, $\rm{D}^{+}/\rm{D}^0$ and $\rm{D}^{*+}/\rm{D}^0$ ratios. For the particular case when the prompt charm hadrons are within jets, all the ratios show modifications relative to the case where the presence of jets is not required. A study in two different ranges of the relative transverse activity is also conducted. In this case only the baryon to meson ratios show a dependence on the underlying event activity. Results indicate that baryon and meson hadronisation are not affected in the same way by local effects or global event conditions

</details>


### [101] [Statistical Inference of Scattering Parameters for Exotic Hadronic States in the $J/ψ\,p$ Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25106)
*André Aimé Atangana Likéné,P. Pradyun Hebbar,Alexis Franck Rothen,Ron Rayan Baatjes,Saralees Nadarajah,Patrice Ele Abiama,Tobias Golling,Germain Hubert Ben-Bolie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 对LHCb Run 1+2数据中J/ψp不变质量谱中的隐藏魅五夸克候选态进行了全局双通道Flatté振幅分析，同时拟合三个五夸克振幅（含多项式背景和复耦合相位），通过有效距离展开提取散射长度和有效距离，并利用非参数自助重采样确定不确定性。研究发现实耦合下散射参数支持分子图像，但引入耦合相位后结论稳健性下降；同时证明紧邻的Pc(4440)+和Pc(4457)+态之间干涉效应显著，揭示了非相干求和近似的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对LHCb发现的隐藏魅五夸克态的分析多采用非相干求和近似，忽略了紧邻态之间的干涉效应，且未同时考虑耦合相位对散射参数提取的影响。本文旨在通过全局双通道Flatté振幅分析，更严格地评估五夸克态的分子性解释，并检验非相干求和近似的有效性。

Method: 采用全局双通道Flatté振幅分析方法，对三个五夸克候选态（Pc(4440)+、Pc(4457)+等）进行同时拟合，振幅中包含多项式背景项和复耦合相位。通过有效距离展开（ERE）从拟合得到的振幅中提取散射长度和有效距离，使用非参数自助重采样方法估计不确定度。

Result: 实耦合情况下散射参数与分子图像假设相容；但当引入复耦合相位后，该结论稳健性明显下降。此外，Pc(4440)+与Pc(4457)+两个紧邻态之间的干涉效应显著，表明传统的非相干求和近似会引入系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 五夸克态是否有分子性的结论高度依赖于振幅模型中对耦合相位和干涉效应的处理；非相干求和近似不足以可靠地描述紧邻五夸克态，需要更精细的全局振幅分析来稳健地判别其内部结构。

Abstract: We present a global two-channel Flatté amplitude analysis of the hidden-charm pentaquark candidates observed by the LHCb collaboration in the J/ψp invariant-mass spectrum using the Run 1+2 dataset. We simultaneously fit the three pentaquark amplitudes to the full spectrum, including a polynomial background and complex coupling phases. The scattering length and effective range are extracted from the fitted amplitudes using the effective range expansion, with uncertainties determined through non-parametric bootstrap resampling. While real couplings yield scattering parameters compatible with a molecular interpretation, this conclusion becomes less robust when coupling phases are included. We further find that interference effects are important for the closely spaced Pc(4440)+ and Pc(4457)+ states, demonstrating the limitations of the incoherent-sum approximation.

</details>


### [102] [Orbital angular momentum at small $x$ in the large $N_c\&N_f$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25120)
*G. Zardo Becker,Yuri V. Kovchegov,Ming Li,Brandon Manley,Andrey Tarasov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在large-Nc&Nf极限下，将质子的夸克与胶子轨道角动量（OAM）分布的小x分析从先前的大Nc极限推广到Nc与Nf同时取大且比值固定的情形。在双对数近似（DLA）下，修正了夸克OAM分布的小x算符表达式，将夸克和胶子OAM分布与极化偶极子振幅的碰撞参数矩联系起来，推导出新的演化方程，并数值求解得到OAM分布与螺旋度分布共享相同的小x截距，截距α_h≈3.48√(α_s N_c/2π)（N_f=N_c=3），小于大Nc极限得到的3.66值。OAM与螺旋度PDF的比值在x→0时约为-1.01和-1.94，且几乎不依赖N_f。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅在大Nc极限下分析了夸克和胶子OAM分布的小x行为，但真实世界中N_c=3且N_f≠0，需要将分析推广到更物理的large-Nc&Nf极限以计入夸克味道数的效应，从而更准确地理解质子自旋结构中小x区域的OAM分布。

Method: 在large-Nc&Nf极限下，采用DLA近似（求和α_s ln²(1/x)的高阶贡献），修正夸克OAM分布的小x算符表达式，将OAM分布表示为极化偶极子振幅的碰撞参数矩；推导新的DLA演化方程（包括一个控制夸克OAM的新矩振幅方程）；对N_f=2,3,4,5,6和N_c=3进行数值求解，并与螺旋度演化联立。

Result: OAM分布与螺旋度分布共享共同的小x截距：L_{q+q̄}~L_G~ΔΣ~ΔG~(1/x)^{α_h}，其中α_h≈3.48√(α_s N_c/2π)（N_f=N_c=3），小于大Nc极限的3.66值。当N_f从2到6变化时，截距均小于大Nc结果。在x→0、Q²=10 GeV²处，L_{q+q̄}/ΔΣ≈-1.01，L_G/ΔG≈-1.94，两个比值几乎不依赖N_f。

Conclusion: 在更多物理的大Nc&Nf极限下，OAM分布与螺旋度分布共享相同的截距，表明它们在红外演化上紧密关联。有限N_f效应会降低截距值。OAM与螺旋度分布的比值约为负1到负2，且对N_f不敏感，说明味道数对OAM相对大小的影响有限。未来工作可进一步考虑NLO修正、数值精度提升及与实验数据的联系。

Abstract: We extend the small-$x$ analysis of the quark and gluon orbital angular momentum (OAM) distributions in the proton from the large-$N_c$ limit considered in our earlier works to the large-$N_c\&N_f$ limit, in which the numbers of quark colors $N_c$ and flavors $N_f$ are large with their ratio held fixed. Working in the double-logarithmic approximation (DLA), summing powers of $α_s \ln^2(1/x)$ with $α_s$ the strong coupling and $x$ the proton's momentum fraction carried by a parton, we correct the small-$x$ operator expression for the quark OAM distribution suggested earlier in \cite{Kovchegov:2024wjs} and relate both the quark and gluon OAM distributions to the impact-parameter moments of the polarized dipole amplitudes. We derive the large-$N_c\&N_f$ evolution equations for these moment amplitudes in the DLA; these include a new equation for the moment amplitude governing the quark OAM. We then solve these evolution equations numerically together with the helicity evolution for $N_f = 2,3,4,5,6$ and $N_c =3$. We find that, similar to the large-$N_c$ case, the OAM distributions share a common small-$x$ intercept with the helicity distributions, $L_{q+\bar{q}}(x,Q^2) \sim L_G(x,Q^2) \sim ΔΣ(x,Q^2) \sim ΔG(x,Q^2) \sim \left(\frac{1}{x} \right)^{α_h}$ with the intercept $α_h \approx {3.48 \sqrt{α_s N_c/2π}}$ for $N_f = N_c = 3$ (cf.~\cite{Borden:2025ehe}): this result, along with $α_h$ for other values of $N_f \neq 0$ that we studied, is smaller than the intercept of $3.66\sqrt{α_s N_c/2π}$ found in the large-$N_c$ limit. We also compute the ratios of the OAM distributions to the helicity parton distribution functions as $x\to 0$, obtaining $L_{q+\bar{q}}(x,Q^2)/ΔΣ(x,Q^2) \approx -1.01$ and $L_G(x,Q^2)/ΔG(x,Q^2) \approx -1.94$ at $Q^2=10\, \mathrm{GeV}^2$, with both ratios being nearly independent of $N_f$.

</details>


### [103] [Multi-messenger signal from QCD-Axion Ultracompact Minihalos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25338)
*Dorian W. P. Amaral,Enrico D. Schiappacasse,Karla Tapia-Rebolledo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由恒星质量原初黑洞（PBH）作为种子、由QCD轴子暗物质组成的超致密迷你晕（UCMHs）的多信使信号。通过次级内落框架和原暴胀Peccei-Quinn场景的回忆丰度与等曲率条件，数值恢复了特征晕轮廓。研究发现：动力学摩擦导致引力波失相，LISA可观测到质量≲10M⊙的PBH；同时引力聚焦的轴子在中子星磁层中共振转换为光子，产生窄带射电信号，绿岸射电望远镜（GBT）可捕获μeV质量范围的QCD轴子带。引力波探测UCMH的存在与结构，射电信号探测其暗物质的粒子本质，联合观测构成混合暗扇区的独特检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由恒星质量PBH作为种子形成的UCMHs的多信使观测信号，旨在同时探测UCMH的结构特征和其QCD轴子暗物质的粒子性质，从而为混合暗物质扇区提供新的观测检验手段。

Method: 采用次级内落框架（secondary-infall framework），结合原暴胀Peccei-Quinn场景下的回忆丰度和等曲率条件，数值恢复特征晕轮廓；对最内层区域采用保守的恒定密度核。研究中子星-PBH旋近系统在致密结构内的引力波和电磁信号，分析动力学摩擦引起的引力波失相以及引力聚焦轴子在中子星磁层中的共振光子转换。

Result: 动力学摩擦产生相对真空双星的引力波失相，LISA可观测到质量≲10M⊙的PBH。对于邻近星系中子星，绿岸射电望远镜GBT的预期灵敏度可捕获μeV质量范围的QCD轴子带，产生窄带射电信号。

Conclusion: 引力波信号探测UCMH的存在和结构，射电信号探测其暗物质的粒子本质，两者的联合观测为混合暗扇区提供了一种独特的诊断性检验手段。

Abstract: We explore the multimessenger signatures from ultracompact minihalos (UCMHs) seeded by stellar-mass primordial black holes (PBHs) and composed of QCD-axion dark matter. Using the secondary-infall framework, with relic-abundance and isocurvature conditions from a pre-inflationary Peccei-Quinn scenario, we numerically recover the characteristic halo profile and adopt a conservative constant-density core for the innermost region. We study the gravitational wave and electromagnetic signatures from neutron-star-PBH inspirals inside these dense structures. Dynamical friction produces a gravitational-wave dephasing relative to the vacuum binary, observable with LISA for primordial black holes with masses $\lesssim\! 10M_\odot$. Simultaneously, gravitationally focused axions can resonantly convert into photons in the neutron-star magnetosphere, producing a narrow-band radio signature. For nearby galactic neutron stars, the projected sensitivity of the Green Bank radio telescope GBT captures the QCD-axion band in the $μ{\rm eV}$ mass range. Gravitational waves therefore probe the presence and structure of the UCMH, while the radio signal probes the particle nature of its dark matter, making their joint observation a distinctive test of a mixed dark sector.

</details>


### [104] [A Rotational Perturbative Correction to Democratic Neutrino Mixing and JUNO Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25430)
*Maibam Ricky Devi,Swaraj Kumar Nanda,Sudhanwa Patra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入微扰项改造民主混合矩阵（DM），使其推导出的混合角与NuFIT 6.1数据及江门中微子实验（JUNO）的最新结果兼容。作者在保持矩阵幺正性的前提下，在(1,2)、(1,3)和(2,3)三个扇区加入旋转微扰，并在每个矩阵元中引入额外实参数，最终分析了在JUNO发现下允许与不允许的纹理结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民主混合矩阵曾被T2K、Double Chooz和Daya Bay等实验证据排除，但作者希望通过对该矩阵进行微扰改造，使其能够与最新的NuFIT 6.1数据和JUNO实验结果相一致，从而重新评估这一理论框架的可行性。

Method: 采用微扰方法：在民主混合矩阵中加入微扰项，通过在(1,2)、(1,3)和(2,3)三个混合扇区引入旋转微扰，并在每个矩阵元素中添加额外实参数来修正混合角，同时确保修正后的矩阵保持幺正性。

Result: 通过微扰修正，民主混合矩阵得以与NuFIT 6.1和JUNO的最新数据兼容，并系统分析了在JUNO发现背景下允许与不允许的纹理结构。

Conclusion: 民主混合矩阵虽然此前被实验排除，但通过合理的幺正微扰（旋转微扰加额外实参数）可以使其与当前中微子振荡实验数据兼容，为这一理论框架提供了新的生命力。

Abstract: In this work, we revamp the democratic mixing matrix (DM) by adding a perturbation term such that the mixing angles derived from it are compatible with the NuFIT 6.1 and recent findings from the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO). To do this, we have incorporated perturbation term in the elements of the mixing matrix such that it does not lose its unitarity. Thus, the democratic mixing matrix, once ruled out by the experimental evidences from T2K, Double Chooz, and Daya Bay, can be modified with rotational perturbation in the (1,2), (1,3), and (2,3) sectors and additional real parameters added in each element of DM. Finally, we analyze the allowed and disallowed textures in light of the JUNO findings.

</details>


### [105] [Particle production in $p$-O collisions at LHC energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25501)
*Chuan Li,Georg Wolschin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了2025年发布的p-O碰撞（√sNN=9.618 TeV）带电强子产额预测与ALICE实验Run 3初步数据，并据此调整模型参数。该三源模型包含胶子-胶子中心源和两个价夸克软胶子碎裂源，采用色玻璃凝聚态描述初始条件，并用相对论扩散模型计算随时间变化的部分热化过程。模型预测了在较大赝快度下，从后向到前向的最大产额振幅随碰撞中心度由外围到中心发生反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证和校准2025年发表的p-O碰撞带电强子产额理论预测，通过对比ALICE Run 3初步实验数据来检验模型的有效性，并改进模型参数以提高预测精度。

Method: 采用三源模型：一个胶子-胶子中心源加两个价夸克软胶子碎裂源；使用色玻璃凝聚态（CGC）描述初始条件；通过相对论扩散模型（RDM）计算随时间演化的部分热化过程，然后将模型预测与ALICE初步数据比较并反演调整参数。

Result: 模型预测与ALICE Run 3初步数据基本一致，经过参数调整后能够更好地描述实验数据；预测了在较大赝快度下最大产额振幅从后向到前向随碰撞由外围向中心发生反转的物理现象。

Conclusion: 该三源模型结合CGC初始条件和相对论扩散热化机制，能够较好地描述p-O碰撞中的带电强子产额，并揭示了赝快度空间中产额振幅反转这一新的物理预言，有待进一步实验验证。

Abstract: We compare our previous predictions for charged-hadron production in $p$-O collisions at an energy of $\sqrt{s_\mathrm{NN}}=9.618$ TeV as published in 2025 with preliminary Run 3 data from ALICE and adapt the model parameters to the data. Our three-source model comprises a gluon-gluon central source and two valence-quark soft-gluon fragmentation sources. We describe the initial conditions using color-glass-condensate states and calculate the time-dependent partial thermalization with a relativistic diffusion model. An inversion of the maximum production amplitude at larger pseudorapidities from backward to forward towards peripheral collisions is predicted.

</details>


### [106] [Next-to-leading power endpoint DIS and PDF factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25502)
*M. Beneke,M. Schnubel,R. Szafron*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在位置空间软共线有效理论（SCET）框架下，研究了深非弹性散射（DIS）在阈值区域 $x \to 1$ 中超越前导幂次（$1-x$）的领头扭度行为，推导了从次前导幂次（NLP）开始的对角部分子散射道的因子化定理，并揭示了阈值附近的部分子分布函数（PDF）在NLP阶成为双尺度对象，同时建立了NLP胶子分布在大 $x$ 下的因子化形式及两种PDF定义间的全阶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能QCD中，研究DIS阈值区域 $x\to 1$ 的非前导幂次贡献对于精确理解PDF的大 $x$ 行为和相应的对数重求和至关重要。此前研究主要集中在前导幂次，而NLP阶的因子化结构尚不清晰，特别是端点发散的处理和PDF在该动力学区域中的定义问题亟待解决。

Method: 采用位置空间软共线有效理论（SCET）框架：1) 推导off-diagonal部分子散射道的NLP因子化定理，将强子张量分解为来自幂次压低流算符的反硬共线贡献和来自次领头拉氏量相互作用的软共线贡献；2) 通过端点因子化关系解决卷积积分中的端点发散；3) 基于重因子化在端点方案中对 $1-x$ 的前导对数进行重求和；4) 在 $\overline{\rm MS}$ 方案的 $d$ 维因子化与端点方案之间建立全阶等价关系。

Result: 证明了端点发散可由端点因子化关系消除，并由此得出阈值附近PDF在NLP阶成为双尺度（two-scale）对象的结论。推导了NLP胶子分布在 $x\to 1$ 时的因子化形式，用硬共线匹配系数和相减的软共线函数表示。分别从 $\overline{\rm MS}$ 方案的 $d$ 维因子化和基于重因子化的端点方案对 $1-x$ 的前导对数进行了重求和，并建立了两种PDF定义之间的全阶关系。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了DIS阈值区域NLP阶的因子化框架，解决了端点发散问题并揭示了PDF在大 $x$ 极限下的双尺度性质和重因子化结构，为后续在高阶精度下系统处理阈值区域QCD可观测量及PDF的端点对数重求和奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) in the threshold region $x\to 1$ beyond leading power in $1-x$ at leading twist. In the framework of position-space soft-collinear effective theory, we derive a factorization theorem for the off-diagonal parton-scattering channel, which starts at next-to-leading power (NLP). The hadronic tensor separates into an anti-hardcollinear contribution from power-suppressed currents and a soft-collinear contribution from subleading Lagrangian interactions, whose convolution integrals exhibit endpoint divergences. We show that these divergences are resolved by endpoint factorization relations, implying that the parton distribution function (PDF) near threshold becomes a two-scale object at NLP. We derive the factorization of the NLP gluon distribution at large $x$ in terms of hardcollinear matching coefficients and a subtracted soft-collinear function. We resum the leading logarithms of $1-x$ both from $d$-dimensional factorization in the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scheme and in an endpoint scheme based on refactorization, and establish the all-order relation between the two definitions of the PDF.

</details>


### [107] [Pair production of $h$ in the $U(1)_X$SSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25504)
*Yue-Tong Liu,Shu-Min Zhao,Meng-Zi Cao,Shuang Di,Rong-Zhi Sun,Xing-Xing Dong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在U(1)_X超对称标准模型（U(1)_XSSM）中，通过胶子融合过程gg→hh产生最轻中性希格斯对的过程。该模型作为MSSM的U(1)扩展，引入新的超场，从而贡献额外的单圈修正。数值分析表明规范耦合g_X和g_YX是最敏感的参数，模型在当前实验约束下产生可观的新物理修正，为高亮度LHC希格斯物理研究和新物理搜索提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯对产生过程是探测希格斯自相互作用和希格斯势结构的重要探针。在超越标准模型的新物理框架（如U(1)_XSSM）中，研究gg→hh过程有助于理解新物理对希格斯对产生的修正效应，为高亮度LHC时代的新物理搜索提供理论预测和指导。

Method: 在U(1)_X超对称标准模型框架下，通过胶子融合过程计算最轻中性希格斯对产生截面gg→hh。模型中引入的新超场贡献额外的单圈振幅，作者对截面进行了数值参数依赖分析，并在二维参数平面上给出等高线分布图。

Result: 数值分析表明，规范耦合g_X和g_YX是影响截面的最敏感参数；在当前实验约束条件下，该模型能够产生可观的新物理修正效应。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于深化对U(1)_XSSM中希格斯物理的理解，并为高亮度LHC上的新物理搜索提供理论指导。

Abstract: Higgs pair production provides an important probe of the Higgs self-interaction and the Higgs potential structure. We study the lightest neutral Higgs pair production process $gg \to hh$ via gluon fusion at the 14 TeV LHC, in the $U(1)_X$ supersymmetric standard model. As a $U(1)$ extension of the minimal supersymmetric standard model (MSSM), this model introduces new superfields that bring additional one-loop contributions to the production amplitude. We analyze the parameter dependence of the cross section numerically and present contour distributions in two-dimensional parameter planes. The results indicate that the gauge couplings $g_X$ and $g_{YX}$ are the most sensitive parameters, and the model yields sizable new physics corrections under current experimental constraints. This work helps to understand Higgs physics in the $U(1)_X$SSM and guides new physics searches at the high-luminosity LHC.

</details>


### [108] [Rare Exclusive Top Decays t , b M and Vector-Meson Helicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25508)
*M. Ahmadi,M. Monemzadeh,N. Tazimi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对稀有顶夸克衰变 t → bM（M为赝标或矢量介子）进行了领头阶研究，基于树级 t → bW* → b(qq̄') 跃迁和因子化近似，推导出振幅和部分宽度表达式，给出分支比预言约为10^-8到10^-7量级，并推导了矢量未态的自旋螺旋度振幅和标准模型极化分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为稀有顶级夸克衰变提供更新的标准模型基准，并作为非标准带电流相互作用的EFT诠释出发点，同时检验这些可观测面对反常手征tWb耦合的敏感性。

Method: 采用树级跃迁t→bW*→b(qq̄')的领头阶计算，使用因子化近似将长距离QCD动力学编码到介子衰变常数f_M中，推导出紧凑的振幅和部分宽度表达式，并导出矢量介子情形下的螺旋度振幅与极化分数。

Result: 对t→bπ⁺、t→bK⁺、t→bD_s^{(*)}、t→bB_c^{(*)}等代表性模式给出分支比预言Br~10^-8–10^-7；提供了矢量末态的标准模型极化分数，并概述了在m_b→0极限下这些可观测量对反常手征tWb耦合的敏感性。

Conclusion: 本工作为稀有顶夸克衰变提供了更新的SM基线，并为EFT框架下非标准带电流相互作用的解释奠定了起点。

Abstract: We present a leading-order study of the rare exclusive decays $t \to bM$, where $M$ denotes a pseudoscalar or vector meson. Starting from the tree-level transition $t \to bW^* \to b(q\bar q')$ and using a factorization approximation, we derive compact expressions for the amplitudes and partial widths in which the long-distance QCD dynamics is encoded in the meson decay constants $f_M$. We provide numerical branching-ratio predictions for representative modes such as $t \to bπ^+$, $t \to bK^+$, $t \to bD_s^{(*)}$, and $t \to bB_c^{(*)}$, finding $\mathrm{Br} \sim 10^{-8}$--$10^{-7}$. For vector final states, we derive helicity amplitudes and Standard-Model polarization fractions, and we outline the sensitivity of these observables to anomalous chiral $tWb$ couplings in the limit $m_b \to 0$. Our results provide an updated Standard Model (SM) baseline for rare exclusive top-quark decays and a starting point for EFT interpretations of non-standard charged-current interactions.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum-Kinetic Leptogenesis and Gravitational Waves from Seesaw-Assisted Domain-Wall Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25590)
*Gayatri Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个最小两右手中微子I型跷跷板模型中共振轻子生成与原初随机引力波背景之间的联系。通过引入实单态标量场，Z2对称的树级势与辐射诱导的真空能偏差相结合，驱动畴壁网络湮灭。作者利用密度矩阵动力学框架描述准简并重中微子系统，并以r_q=ΔM/Γ_N1为组织变量区分分离、共振和相干三个区域，建立了重中微子参数、量子动力学重子不对称性与引力波峰频率/振幅之间的模型依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索共振轻子生成与随机引力波背景之间的理论联系，期望引力波信号能作为早期宇宙物理的探针，并为轻子生成机制提供观测约束。

Method: 采用最小两右手中微子I型跷跷板模型，引入实单态标量构造Z2对称的树级势；利用辐射修正产生真空能偏差导致畴壁湮灭；对准简并重中微子采用密度矩阵量子动力学框架，以r_q=ΔM/Γ_N1作为组织变量区分各动力学区域；将控制物理质量劈裂的重中微子参数与辐射生成畴壁偏差关联起来。

Result: 揭示引力波信号并非对单个低能中微子参数的直接测量，而是提供了一种模型依赖的相容性关系，将中微子数据、共振轻子生成、畴壁湮灭和随机引力波背景联系起来。

Conclusion: 在该模型中，引力波信号可以作为共振轻子生成过程的间接探针，但需要结合多种观测数据建立一致性检验，而不能单独用于确定中微子参数。

Abstract: We investigate the connection between resonant leptogenesis and a primordial stochastic gravitational-wave background in a minimal two-right-handed-neutrino type-I seesaw with a real singlet scalar. The scalar sector admits a $\mathbb Z_2$-symmetric tree-level potential, while the tiny right-handed-neutrino coupling to the $\mathbb Z_2$-odd scalar generates a radiative vacuum-energy bias that causes the domain-wall network to annihilate. For quasi-degenerate heavy neutrinos, we describe the heavy-neutrino system with a density-matrix kinetic framework and use the ratio $r_q=ΔM/Γ_{N_1}$ as an organizing variable for the separated, resonant and coherent regimes. The heavy-neutrino parameters that control the physical mass splitting also enter the radiatively generated domain-wall bias, providing a model-dependent link between the quantum-kinetic baryon asymmetry and the gravitational-wave peak frequency and amplitude. We emphasize that the gravitational- wave signal is not a direct measurement of an individual low-energy neutrino parameter; rather, it provides a model-dependent consistency relation among neutrino data, resonant leptogenesis, domain-wall annihilation and the stochastic gravitational-wave background.

</details>


### [110] [A Comprehensive Analysis of the R2HDM Vacuum Evolution and the Induced GW and Collider Phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25636)
*Lisa Biermann,Christoph Borschensky,Rafael Boto,Margarete Mühlleitner,Rui Santos,Joao Viana*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用BSMPTv3代码系统分析CP守恒的2-Higgs-Doublet模型（2HDM）中多次强一级相变产生的引力波信号，并考虑了所有理论与实验约束以及不确定性讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越标准模型（BSM）的扩展Higgs扇区可以通过电弱重子生成机制解释宇宙的重子不对称性，而所需的强一级相变会产生可在LISA等未来实验中探测的引力波信号。要最终确定具体的底层物理，必须深入理解模型真空的演化以及引力波谱推导中的各种不确定性。

Method: 使用自研代码BSMPTv3可靠地推导具有多个真空方向的扩展Higgs扇区的有限温度真空结构，计算（多个）强一级相变的引力波谱，并应用于CP守恒的2-Higgs-Doublet模型。在考虑所有相关理论和实验约束的基础上，对真空演化、对撞机与引力波现象学进行系统分析，并辅以不确定性讨论。

Result: 对CP守恒2HDM的真空演化进行了详尽分析，计算了相关（可能的多次）强一级一级相变产生的引力波谱，评估了这些信号在LISA等未来实验中的可探测性，并给出了推导过程中的不确定性评估。

Conclusion: 通过结合精确的真空结构计算、对撞机约束与引力波现象学分析，BSMPTv3为通过引力波探测探索BSM物理提供了可靠工具，同时强调了理解模型真空演化与理论不确定性对于最终识别底层物理机制至关重要。

Abstract: Extended Higgs sectors beyond the Standard Model (BSM) allow to dynamically generate the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe through electroweak baryogenesis and thereby solve one of the most prominent open problems of the SM. The strong first-order phase transitions (PTs), required to preserve the generated asymmetry in the electroweak vacuum, source gravitational waves (GW) that can be tested at future experiments like LISA. Gravitational waves hence provide the exciting possibility to probe BSM physics through cosmological processes. In order to be able to eventually pin down the specific underlying physics, a good understanding of the evolution of the vacuum of the model under investigation is indispensable as well as of the uncertainties that are involved in the derivation of the GW spectrum. We use our code BSMPTv3 that allows to reliably derive the finite temperature vacuum structure of extended Higgs sectors with multiple vacuum directions and calculates the GW spectrum of the found (multiple) strong first-order PTs, and we apply it to the real, i.e. CP-conserving, 2-Higgs-Doublet Model. Taking into account all relevant theoretical and experimental constraints, we perform a thorough analysis of its vacuum evolution, the related collider and GW phenomenology and complement it by an uncertainty discussion.

</details>


### [111] [Impact of model-independent higher-order contributions to the Higgs-boson self-couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25705)
*Johannes Braathen,Wrishik Naskar,Georg Weiglein*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的模型无关的更高阶贡献，涉及已探测到的希格斯玻色子的三线性与四线性自耦合，这些贡献可以一致地纳入现有的一圈或二圈预言中，从而改善在特定模型中获得的理论预测，并能显著改变对三线性与四线性希格斯自耦合的推断界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前标准模型之外的特定模型中，三线性和四线性希格斯自耦合的预言仅计算到一圈或某些情况下两圈水平，缺少对可能具有大效应的模型无关更高阶贡献的系统性处理，这可能导致对当前及未来实验测量的解释不够可靠。

Method: 识别由插入圈修正后的三线性与四线性希格斯自耦合值所构成的"类标准模型"图所代表的模型无关更高阶贡献，将其一致地纳入现有的一圈或两圈预言中，并结合实验数据与理论约束对三线性和四线性自耦合进行同时拟合，同时提供了实现这些效应的公开工具。

Result: 这些新的模型无关更高阶贡献可在推定的希格斯自耦合界限上产生显著偏移，例如将三线性自耦合修饰因子的理论上限从实验限制的6.1移至约4.9，表明纳入这些修正对可靠解释当前与未来测量至关重要。

Conclusion: 将这些模型无关的高阶贡献纳入考虑是可靠解释当前及未来希格斯自耦合测量所必需的，并公开提供了相应工具以便与实验限制作直接比较。

Abstract: We point out the relevance of a new class of model-independent higher-order contributions to the trilinear and quartic self-couplings of the detected Higgs boson giving rise to potentially large effects of physics beyond the Standard Model (SM). It consists of SM-like diagrams with insertions of loop-corrected values for the trilinear and quartic Higgs self-couplings. While up to now predictions in specific models of physics beyond the SM were only evaluated up to the one-loop or, in some cases, up to the two-loop level, we demonstrate how potentially large higher-order contributions can consistently be incorporated into existing one-loop or two-loop predictions. These model-independent higher-order contributions therefore improve the existing predictions that were obtained in specific models. Confronting our predictions with the relevant experimental results and theoretical constraints, we perform a simultaneous determination of the trilinear and quartic Higgs-boson self-couplings. We show that the new set of model-independent higher-order contributions can induce sizeable shifts in the inferred bounds on the trilinear and quartic Higgs self-couplings. For example, including the new higher-order corrections can shift the upper bound on the trilinear self-coupling modifier entering the theoretical predictions to $\sim4.9$ from the experimental limit of $ 6.1$. Our results demonstrate that incorporating these higher-order contributions is essential for a reliable interpretation of present and future measurements. We further provide a public tool that implements these effects and enables direct comparison with experimental limits.

</details>


### [112] [Odderon in the diffractive dip region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25713)
*E. G. S. Luna,M. G. Ryskin,V. A. Khoze*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Regge-inspired参数化方法，利用质子-质子和质子-反质子弹性散射的衍射凹陷区域数据（√s>500 GeV, 0.2<|t|<1 GeV²），检验C宇称奇（C-odd）效应。研究发现拟合不需要稳定的非零C-odd项，即Odderon贡献与零兼容或约束较弱，即使增加额外的Pomeron项或第二Odderon项也无法改变这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验高能弹性散射中是否存在C-odd（Odderon）效应的实验证据，并对近期基于标度性和解析性得到的Odderon振幅进行补充检验。衍射凹陷区域是探测C-odd效应的敏感区域，而此前相关工作大多是通过TOTEM数据得到的。

Method: 使用简单的Regge-inspired参数化方法拟合高能弹性散射数据：1）C-even振幅由三个有效Pomeron-like项描述；2）C-odd扇区分别用多种单Odderon-like形式测试；3）相对相位由对应偶/奇贡献的符号因子固定，而非自由参数，从而通过crossing联系各项的实部和虚部；4）进一步测试添加第四个Pomeron-like项或第二个Odderon-like项的影响。

Result: 拟合结果显示：1）数据不需要稳定的非零C-odd项，奇数贡献与零兼容或约束较弱；2）添加第四个Pomeron-like项带来的改进主要是C-even的；3）第二个Odderon-like项无法被解析为独立贡献；4）C-odd项在最佳拟合中倾向于消失。

Conclusion: 在衍射凹陷区域，现有pp和ppbar弹性散射数据无法为Odderon效应提供稳定的独立证据。这为近期声称观测到Odderon振幅的结论提供了一个补充性的对比检验，表明C-odd信号在该分析框架下并未表现出显著的统计必要性。

Abstract: We examine possible $C$-odd effects in elastic proton-proton and proton-antiproton scattering in the diffractive dip region. Collider data with $\sqrt{s}>500$ GeV are fitted for $0.2<|t|<1~\GeV^2$, using a simple Regge-inspired parametrization of effective $C$-even and $C$-odd amplitudes. The relative phases are not left free, but are fixed by the signature factors of the corresponding even and odd contributions. In this way, the real and imaginary parts of each term are linked by crossing. The $C$-even amplitude is first described by three effective Pomeron-like terms. The $C$-odd sector is then tested with several one-term Odderon-like forms. We find that the fits do not require a stable nonzero $C$-odd term: the odd contribution is either compatible with zero or becomes weakly constrained when we allow extra freedom. We then test whether the conclusion is changed by adding either a fourth Pomeron-like term or a second Odderon-like term. The improvement obtained with the fourth Pomeron-like term is mainly $C$-even, while the second Odderon-like term is not resolved as an independent contribution. These results provide a complementary test of recent determinations of the Odderon amplitude based on scaling and analyticity.

</details>


### [113] [Low-$x$ parton densities accounting for absorptive effects and exclusive $J/ψ$ and $Υ$ data from the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25724)
*V. Bertone,C. A. Flett,A. D. Martin,M. G. Ryskin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文基于HERA的深度非弹性散射数据和LHC上测量的J/ψ及Υ截面，研究吸收修正对低x区域部分子分布函数（PDFs）的影响。利用xFitter框架，在NLO和NNLO^*方法下探讨了非线性演化对PDF的影响，并确定了质子热点的有效尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低x和高能区域，线性DGLAP演化可能不足以描述PDFs的行为，因为饱和效应和吸收修正变得重要。作者希望通过结合DIS和重夸克偶素（J/ψ、Υ）的衍射数据，量化这些非线性效应对全球PDF确定的影响，并为未来更精确的PDF全局拟合提供指导。

Method: 使用xFitter全局拟合框架，联合HERA的DIS数据和LHC上的J/ψ、Υ排他产生截面数据。在NLO和前一工作引入的NNLO^*方法下，对比线性与非线性演化对PDFs的影响，并利用吸收修正框架提取质子热点的有效尺寸参数。

Result: 研究表明吸收修正（非线性效应）在低x区域对PDFs有显著影响，尤其是在低到中等标度下。通过此框架确定了质子热点的有效尺寸，为理解胶子饱和动力学提供了约束，并指出了对全球PDF获取的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将排他重夸克偶素数据纳入PDF全局拟合中可以有效约束低x区域的非线性行为，同时为质子内部结构（热点评定）提供了新的认识，对未来全球PDF确定具有重要的方法论意义。

Abstract: Based on the combined Deep-Inelastic Scattering (DIS) data from HERA and the exclusive $J/ψ$ and $Υ$ cross sections measured at the LHC, we investigate the role of absorptive corrections on the low-$x$ behaviour of parton distribution functions (PDFs) at low and moderate scales. Using $\texttt{xFitter}$, we study the impact of these data and of non-linear evolution on PDFs at next-to-leading order (NLO) and through the NNLO$^*$ method introduced in a previous work. Finally, we determine the effective size of proton hot spots within this framework and discuss the implications of our results for future global PDF determinations.

</details>


### [114] [Deeply Inelastic Scattering Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25790)
*Felix Hekhorn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文以深度非弹性散射（DIS）为例，系统讨论了微扰QCD框架下从数学问题到运动学修正、再到实验测量解释的一系列主题，并介绍了将这些特征全部实现的开源代码Yadism，该代码已用于从全球完全非弹性DIS数据提取部分子分布函数（PDFs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIS因其概念简单常作为QCD和核子部分子结构的教科书范例，但其所谓的简单性恰恰使其成为讨论诸多额外效应和修正的理想案例。作者希望通过系统阐述这些效应和修正，帮助读者理解DIS的理论与实验之间的复杂关联，并展示如何将这些特征纳入实际的PDF提取中。

Method: 本文以讲座形式组织内容，在微扰QCD领域内系统覆盖DIS相关的数学问题、运动学修正及实验测量解释的实际挑战；同时介绍了开源代码Yadism，该代码实现了所有讨论的特征，并已用于基于广泛世界数据的完全非弹性DIS的PDF提取。

Result: 所有讨论的理论特征均已在开源代码Yadism中实现，该代码已被成功用于从涵盖广泛的全球完全非弹性DIS实验数据中提取部分子分布函数，验证了所讨论的效应和修正方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 虽然论文以DIS作为示例，但所讨论的主题（包括数学方法、运动学修正和实验挑战）对更广泛的过程类别也具有相关性，为理解和提取核子部分子结构提供了系统框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Due to its conceptual simplicity, Deeply Inelastic Scattering (DIS) often serves as a textbook example for Quantum Chromo Dynamics (QCD) and partonic structure of nucleons. However, it is this supposed simplicity, which makes DIS an ideal case for discussing many additional effects and corrections. In these lectures we discuss DIS within the domain of perturbative QCD covering a range of topics ranging from mathematical problems to kinematical corrections, and to practical challenges when attempting to interpret experimental measurements. Although here we focus on the exemplary case of DIS, the discussed topcis are relevant to a larger class of processes. We also give an overview of existing and future DIS measurements, and discuss their implementation into modern extractions of parton distribution functions (PDFs) which quantify the partonic structure of nucleons.
  The discussed features are all implemented in the open source code Yadism, which has been used for PDF extractions from a wide range of the available world data on fully inclusive DIS.

</details>


### [115] [How elastic unitarity governs resonance peaks and residue phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25802)
*S. Ceci,R. Omerović,H. Osmanović,M. Uroić,B. Zauner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过弹性幺正性对共振振幅施加约束，推导出一个将反应阈值、S矩阵极点和Breit-Wigner峰联系起来的几何规则。该规则在从$^5$He到Higgs玻色子的六个数量级能量范围内得到验证，并预测了多个共振态未知的残差相位，还可逆推$\Upsilon(4S)$的极点位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共振态是粒子物理和核物理中的核心概念，但S矩阵极点、Breit-Wigner峰和反应阈值之间的关系长期以来缺乏统一的几何描述。作者希望找到一种普适的规则来连接这些观测量，从而能够预测未知的共振参数（如残差相位）并从实验峰位反推极点位置。

Method: 作者将弹性幺正性条件施加于共振振幅上，推导出连接反应阈值、S矩阵极点与Breit-Wigner峰的几何规则。他们先在多个能量尺度的共振态上（从$^5$He到Higgs玻色子，跨越六个数量级的能量范围）验证了这一规则的有效性，然后利用该规则预测未知的残差相位，并通过反转该形式体系从实验上观测到的$\Upsilon(4S)$峰位确定其极点。

Result: 该规则在从$^5$He到Higgs玻色子的广泛能量范围内得到验证。预测结果包括：$^5$He的残差相位为$-44(12)^\circ$，$\Sigma(1385)^+$为$-24(7)^\circ$，$\Xi(1530)^0$为$-7(13)^\circ$。通过反转该形式体系，确定$\Upsilon(4S)$的极点为$10575(1)-i\,8.3(13)$ MeV，相位为$-52(6)^\circ$。

Conclusion: 弹性幺正性导出的几何规则为共振参数之间建立了普适联系，不仅能够在极广的能量范围内得到验证，还能用于预测未知的残差相位和从实验数据反推极点参数，为共振态分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Imposing elastic unitarity on resonant amplitudes yields a geometric rule connecting the reaction threshold, S-matrix pole, and Breit-Wigner peak. Validated across six orders of magnitude in energy, from $^5\text{He}$ to the Higgs boson, this rule predicts currently unknown residue phases: $-44(12)^\circ$ for $^5\mathrm{He}$, $-24(7)^\circ$ for $Σ(1385)^+$, and $-7(13)^\circ$ for $Ξ(1530)^0$. By inverting this formalism, we determine the $Υ(4S)$ pole from its empirical peak to be $10575(1)-i\,8.3(13)$ MeV, with a $-52(6)^\circ$ phase.

</details>


### [116] [Mixing effects in radiative decays of heavy-strange axial-vector mesons within light-cone QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25843)
*T. M. Aliev,S. Bilmis,M. Savci*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用光锥QCD求和规则计算了奇异重轴矢量介子$D_{s1}(2460)$、$D_{s1}(2536)$、$B_{s1}(5750)$和$B_{s1}(5830)$向基态赝标量介子（$D_s$或$B_s$）辐射衰变的宽度，并考虑了$^{1}P_1$与$^{3}P_1$构型之间的混合效应。结果表明$D_{s1}(2460)\to D_s\gamma$的衰变宽度显著大于$D_{s1}(2536)\to D_s\gamma$，这一差异源于两种自旋构型的干涉效应（低激发态为相长干涉，高激发态为相消干涉）；而在底奇异扇区，两个衰变宽度在误差范围内相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异重轴矢量介子的自旋态混合（$^{1}P_1$-$^{3}P_1$）对其辐射衰变性质有重要影响，理解这一混合效应有助于揭示重夸克有效理论框架下轻夸克自旋耦合与轨道角动量的非微扰结构。此前对这类过程的理论计算尚不完善，需要通过QCD求和规则等非微扰方法给出可靠预言。

Method: 采用光锥QCD求和规则（light-cone QCD sum rules）计算辐射衰变宽度，在计算中显式纳入$^{1}P_1$和$^{3}P_1$两个构型的混合。在底奇异扇区，将实验上已确立的$B_{s1}(5830)$与理论上预期存在的更低$1^+$态$B_{s1}(5750)$同时纳入分析。

Result: 得到四个衰变宽度：$\Gamma[D_{s1}(2460)\to D_s\gamma]=45.9^{+10.1}_{-8.5}$ keV，$\Gamma[D_{s1}(2536)\to D_s\gamma]=0.55^{+1.73}_{-0.48}$ keV，$\Gamma[B_{s1}(5750)\to B_s\gamma]=12.0^{+2.9}_{-2.3}$ keV，$\Gamma[B_{s1}(5830)\to B_s\gamma]=8.9^{+2.4}_{-2.0}$ keV。在粲奇异扇区，低激发态的衰变宽度比高激发态大一个量级以上；在底奇异扇区，两者在误差范围内可比。

Conclusion: 奇异重轴矢量介子的辐射衰变模式对两种自旋构型（$^{1}P_1$与$^{3}P_1$）的混合非常敏感：低激发态中基流贡献相长干涉导致较大宽度，高激发态中相消干涉导致宽度被压低。该结果表明辐射衰变可作为探测自旋混合结构的灵敏探针。

Abstract: We calculate the radiative decay widths of $D_{s1}(2460)\to D_sγ$, $D_{s1}(2536)\to D_sγ$, $B_{s1}(5750)\to B_sγ$, and $B_{s1}(5830)\to B_sγ$ within light-cone QCD sum rules, taking into account the mixing between the $^{1}P_{1}$ and $^{3}P_{1}$ configurations. In the bottom-strange sector, we consider the experimentally established $B_{s1}(5830)$ together with the theoretically expected lower $1^+$ state, denoted $B_{s1}(5750)$. We obtain $Γ[D_{s1}(2460)\to D_sγ]=45.9^{+10.1}_{-8.5}$ keV, $Γ[D_{s1}(2536)\to D_sγ]=0.55^{+1.73}_{-0.48}$ keV, $Γ[B_{s1}(5750)\to B_sγ]=12.0^{+2.9}_{-2.3}$ keV, and $Γ[B_{s1}(5830)\to B_sγ]=8.9^{+2.4}_{-2.0}$ keV. The $D_{s1}(2460)\to D_sγ$ decay width is substantially larger than that of $D_{s1}(2536)\to D_sγ$. This difference originates from $^{1}P_{1}$-$^{3}P_{1}$ mixing: the two basis-current contributions interfere constructively for the lower state and destructively for the higher state. In the bottom-strange sector, the two widths are comparable within their uncertainties, although the higher state still exhibits destructive interference. These results show that the radiative decay pattern of strange heavy axial-vector mesons is sensitive to the mixing of the two spin configurations.

</details>


### [117] [Imaginary Rotation Breaks Charge Conjugation in Hot QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25849)
*Rin Takada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从颜色单态物理态空间上的恒等式出发，推导出虚转动与虚夸克化学势之间的密度算符恒等式，将SU(3) QCD中(Ω_I/T,θ_q)=(2π,0)的虚转动系统精确映射到Roberge-Weiss点(0,π)上。作者在单圈有效势框架下解析地得到了无质量三味夸克情形下二阶转变点和一阶锁相转变点的精确值，并讨论了无质量近似下的连续简并性及有限奇异夸克质量对该简并的消除，最后给出可在格点QCD中检验这些精确关系的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立虚转动QCD与Roberge-Weiss点之间的精确对应关系，并通过解析方法确定虚转动诱导的相变临界点，为格点QCD中的数值验证提供理论预言。

Method: 利用恒等式 e^{2πiJ_z}=(-1)^F=e^{iπQ} 在颜色单态物理态空间上成立的性质，推导密度算符恒等式 ρ(Ω_I/T+2π,θ_q)=ρ(Ω_I/T,θ_q+π)。进一步在SU(3) Weyl alcove上通过对单圈有效势进行极值化，解析求解相变临界点。

Result: (1) 建立了虚转动与虚化学势之间的精确映射，证明(2π,0)点等价于Roberge-Weiss点(0,π)；(2) 对三味无质量夸克解析得到二阶转变点 Ω_{I,C}/(πT)=(22-2√22)/9 以及一阶锁相转变点 Ω_{I,lock}/(πT)=2/3+2√111/27；(3) 指出无质量单圈近似下连续简并是偶然的，有限奇异夸克质量会消除它。

Conclusion: 这些精确关系和轴上预言可以在格点QCD中通过特定条件进行数值检验，该框架为理解虚转动下的QCD相结构提供了无模型、无近似的严格基础。

Abstract: Starting from the identity $\mathrm{e}^{2π\mathrm{i}J_z}=(-1)^{F}=\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}πQ}$ for the angular momentum, fermion parity, and quark number operators, valid on the color-singlet physical state space, we derive a density-operator identity that ties imaginary rotation to an imaginary quark chemical potential, $ρ(Ω_I/T+2π,θ_q)=ρ(Ω_I/T,θ_q+π)$. It follows that the imaginarily rotating system of $SU(3)$ QCD at $(Ω_I/T,θ_q)=(2π,0)$ is mapped exactly onto the Roberge-Weiss point $(0,π)$, where charge conjugation is spontaneously broken above the Roberge-Weiss endpoint. This conclusion depends on no model and no approximation. Minimizing the one-loop effective potential on the rotation axis over the entire $SU(3)$ Weyl alcove, we further obtain analytically, for three massless quark flavors, the second-order transition point $Ω_{I,C}/(πT)=(22-2\sqrt{22})/9$ at which $\mathrm{Im}\,L$ rises continuously, and the first-order transition point $Ω_{I,\mathrm{lock}}/(πT)=2/3+2\sqrt{111}/27$ at which the minimum locks into the nontrivial center elements. In the massless one-loop approximation a continuous degeneracy appears in full QCD and in pure Yang-Mills theory alike, but on the full-QCD side it is merely accidental, lifted by a finite strange-quark mass. Finally, we state the conditions under which the exact relations and the on-axis predictions can be tested in lattice QCD.

</details>


### [118] [Robust Finite-Momentum Instabilities in Dense Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25857)
*André G. da Silva,Ricardo L. S. Farias,Theo Motta,William R. Tavares*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在双味Nambu--Jona-Lasinio模型中，传统紫外正则化方案（三维截断、Pauli--Villars、固有时间）产生的非均匀手征相不稳定区域差异巨大，但将正则化限制于真正发散的真空贡献后，三种方案得到几乎一致的结果，表明空间调制的手征关联是致密介质的固有性质而非紫外方案的产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均匀手征相（如moat regime和有限动量不稳定区）在不同紫外正则化方案下预测范围差异极大，这种方案依赖性严重削弱了有效模型预测的可信度，亟需厘清其根源并建立方案无关的稳健性结论。

Method: 在双味NJL模型中，系统比较三种正则化方案（三维截断、Pauli--Villars、固有时间）对非均匀手征相不稳定区域的影响；分析发现的方案依赖性源于对有限介质贡献（费米面响应）的错误正则化，因此将正则化限制于真正发散的真空贡献，重新计算稳定性图。

Result: 将紫外正则化限定于真发散的真空贡献后，三种正规化方案给出的moat regime起始点和有限动量不稳定边界几乎一致，定性和定量层面均变得稳健；原方案依赖纯粹来自对有限费米面响应介质贡献的不当正则化。

Conclusion: 空间调制的手征关联（非均匀手征相）是致密介质的真实固有性质，而非特定紫外正则化方案的人为产物；正确区分发散真空项与有限介质项是实现方案无关预测的关键。

Abstract: The predicted extent of inhomogeneous chiral phases in effective models of quantum chromodynamics is notoriously sensitive to ultraviolet regularization. We show that this sensitivity is largely artificial. In the two-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model, conventional implementations of three-dimensional cutoff, Pauli--Villars, and proper-time regularization produce strongly different finite-momentum instability regions. Once ultraviolet regulators are restricted to genuinely divergent vacuum contributions, however, all three prescriptions yield nearly identical stability diagrams. Both the onset of the moat regime and the subsequent finite-momentum instability become quantitatively robust. The apparent scheme dependence originates from regulating ultraviolet-finite medium contributions associated with the Fermi-surface response. Our results identify spatially modulated chiral correlations as a genuine property of the dense medium rather than an artifact of the ultraviolet prescription.

</details>


### [119] [Jet Quenching Meets Gluon Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25983)
*Paul Caucal,Kevin Eisenberg,Yacine Mehtar-Tani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大能量对数重求和的理论框架，用于描述重离子碰撞中的喷注碎裂过程。利用喷注淬灭中的尺度层级，揭示了喷注函数遵循Banfi-Marchesini-Smye演化方程，其中介质诱导能量损失和颜色退相干编码在初始条件中，并与饱和物理建立了紧密对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子碰撞中的喷注淬灭是理解夸克-胶子等离子体的关键探针，但现有的理论框架难以统一描述真空辐射、介质诱导辐射和颜色相干效应。需要建立一个新的理论框架，能够从第一性原理出发对这些效应进行统一的微扰描述。

Method: 利用喷注淬灭的尺度层级（能量/角度标度分离），通过重求和大能量对数，推导出喷注函数满足Banfi-Marchesini-Smye（BMS）演化方程。介质效应通过初始条件编码，包括能量损失和颜色退相干。作为原理验证，计算了喷注核修正因子R_AA。

Result: 建立了喷注淬灭与饱和物理之间的对应关系：相干角被识别为饱和尺度Q_s的类比物，并在非线性演化下表现出相同的渐近标度行为。喷注核修正因子的计算定量展示了真空辐射与介质诱导颜色退相干之间的竞争。

Conclusion: 该框架为真空类部分子簇射、介质诱导辐射和颜色相干效应提供了统一的微扰论描述，为在RHIC和LHC上开展喷注淬灭的精确研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a theoretical framework for jet fragmentation in heavy-ion collisions based on the resummation of large energy logarithms. Exploiting the hierarchy of scales characteristic of jet quenching, we show that the jet function obeys the Banfi-Marchesini-Smye evolution equation, with medium-induced energy loss and color decoherence encoded in the initial condition. This structure reveals a close correspondence with saturation physics. In particular, the coherence angle emerges as the analog of the saturation scale and exhibits the same asymptotic scaling behavior under nonlinear evolution. As a proof of principle, we compute the jet nuclear modification factor to quantify the interplay between vacuum radiation and medium-induced color decoherence. Our framework provides a unified perturbative description of vacuum-like parton showers, medium-induced radiation, and color-coherence effects, paving the way for precision studies of jet quenching at RHIC and the LHC.

</details>


### [120] [Color Coherence and the Soft Structure of QCD Jets in Vacuum and the QGP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25984)
*Paul Caucal,Yacine Mehtar-Tani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架，通过对大能量对数进行重求和，统一描述了QCD喷注在真空和夸克-胶子等离子体中的演化。该框架将喷注观测量的阈值行为表达为满足BMS演化的Wilson线关联函数，揭示了软辐射对喷注内部颜色结构的探测机制，并建立了非线性演化方程的层级来刻画颜色相干性和去相干能量损失的演化。在介质中，诱导相互作用仅修改演化的边界条件而不改变其紫外结构。在大N_c极限下，该演化与Balitsky-Kovchegov方程紧密相关，介质相干角与饱和尺度具有类比关系及相同的渐近标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的喷注淬灭理论缺乏对颜色相干效应的统一理解，真空中的类辐射和介质诱导的能量损失通常被分开处理。本文旨在建立一个统一的微扰框架，将喷注演化、颜色相干性和介质效应纳入同一形式体系中，并弥合喷注淬灭与高能QCD饱和物理之间的概念鸿沟。

Method: 利用喷注硬标度与能量损失能量标度之间的强层级分离，通过重求和大的能量对数，将喷注观测量在阈值附近表达为Wilson线关联函数。推导出满足BMS演化的非线性方程层级，并分析介质诱导相互作用对演化边界条件的修正，同时在大N_c极限下建立与Balitsky-Kovchegov方程的联系。

Result: 建立了统一的喷注演化框架，证明软辐射对喷注颜色结构的解析导致非线性演化方程的层级；介质效应仅改变演化边界条件而保持紫外结构不变；在大N_c极限下演化方程与BK方程相关，介质相干角具有与饱和尺度类似的渐近标度行为。

Conclusion: 该框架为喷注淬灭中颜色相干效应的研究建立了微扰理论基础，提供了真空与稠密QCD物质中软喷注演化的统一图像，并将饱和物理的概念成功移植到喷注淬灭领域。

Abstract: We formulate a theoretical framework for the evolution of QCD jets in vacuum and in the quark--gluon plasma through the resummation of large energy logarithms. Exploiting the strong hierarchy between the hard scale of the jet and the energy scale associated with jet energy loss, we show that jet observables near threshold can be formulated in terms of Wilson-line correlators obeying Banfi-Marchesini-Smye (BMS) evolution. In this description, soft radiation resolves the internal color structure of the jet, leading to a hierarchy of non-linear evolution equations that govern the evolution of color coherence and the emergence of decoherent energy loss. For jets propagating through a QCD medium, we demonstrate that medium-induced interactions modify the boundary conditions of the evolution while leaving its ultraviolet structure unchanged. This separation of scales provides a unified description of vacuum-like radiation, medium-induced energy loss, and color coherence. In the large-$N_c$ limit, the resulting evolution is closely related to the Balitsky-Kovchegov equation of high-energy QCD, allowing concepts from saturation physics to be applied to jet quenching. In particular, the medium coherence angle plays a role analogous to the saturation scale and acquires the same asymptotic scaling behavior under evolution. Our framework establishes a perturbative foundation for the study of color coherence effects in jet quenching and provides a unified picture of soft jet evolution in vacuum and in dense QCD matter.

</details>


### [121] [Strong CP and the PMNS Phase in Dirac Left-Right Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26063)
*Vladimir Tello*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究左右对称理论中广义宇称（parity）限制下，强CP相位与轻子CP破坏之间的关联。在狄拉克中微子实现中，对轻子Yukawa矩阵施加扇区实性条件后，轻子CP破坏从独立输入变为分支依赖的预测。理论推导了PMNS Jarlskog不变量与最轻中微子质量和宇称破坏参数ε的解析关系，发现在压缩混合符号狄拉克谱中，微小宇称形变可被放大为阶数一的CP破坏。同时夸克重建提供了ε与强CP角θ̄之间的可计算转换，从而建立起轻子CP、绝对中微子质量标度和强CP之间的相关性族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 左右对称理论中，广义宇称约束使裸QCD角为零，但物理强CP相位θ̄与宇称破坏参数ε相关联，需要寻找轻子区与夸克区之间的联系来完整理解CP破坏的来源及其可观测关联。

Method: 在狄拉克中微子实现下，对狄拉克轻子Yukawa矩阵施加扇区实性条件，要求右手轻子混合矩阵与左手矩阵的复共轭重相位等价。通过构造Jarlskog型CP奇偶不变量的消失条件来分析兼容性，并在物理狄拉克质量层级下，利用固定振荡数据和离散轻子分支，数值求解PMNS Jarlskog不变量作为最轻中微子质量和ε的函数，同时推导领先级解析行为。

Result: 对于三代一般Yukawa，兼容性等价于单一Jarlskog型CP奇偶不变量的消失。在压缩混合符号狄拉克谱中，小的有符号质量求和可将微小宇称形变放大为归一化PMNS Jarlskog不变量的阶数一数值，可达到最大CP破坏。夸克重建独立地提供了ε与θ̄之间可计算的、分支依赖的转换关系。

Conclusion: 左右对称理论框架下，扇区实性条件将轻子CP破坏从独立参数转变为可预测量，并与中微子绝对质量标度和强CP角之间建立了明确的相关性，为检验这类模型提供了新的可观测预言。

Abstract: Left--right symmetric theories with generalized parity restrict the bare QCD angle to a CP-conserving value and relate the physical strong-CP phase \(\barθ\) to a CP-odd parity-breaking parameter \(\eps\). We show that, in the Dirac-neutrino realization, imposing a sectorial reality condition on the Dirac lepton Yukawa matrices turns observable leptonic CP violation from an independent input into a branch-dependent prediction. Parity reconstructs the right-handed leptonic mixing matrix, whereas leptonic reality requires it to be rephasing-equivalent to the complex conjugate of the left-handed one. For generic three-generation Yukawas, compatibility is equivalent to the vanishing of a single Jarlskog-type CP-odd invariant. In the physical Dirac hierarchy, for fixed oscillation data, mass ordering, and discrete leptonic branch, compatibility determines the PMNS Jarlskog invariant as a function of the lightest neutrino mass and \(\eps\). We derive its leading analytic behavior and obtain the nonlinear compatibility branches numerically. In compressed mixed-sign Dirac-neutrino spectra, small signed mass sums can amplify a tiny parity deformation into order-one values of the normalized PMNS Jarlskog invariant, including maximal CP violation. The corresponding quark reconstruction independently provides a calculable, branch-dependent conversion between \(\eps\) and \(\barθ\). Together, the leptonic and quark relations define a family of correlations among leptonic CP violation, the absolute neutrino mass scale, and strong CP.

</details>

### [122] [Exact topology of conservative multiplicative cascades: An ultrametric transfer-operator genus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24897)
*Cristiano G. Sabiu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上精确预测了守恒型（微正则）随机排列级联的拓扑结构，给出了二维级联水平集跳跃集的数字（立方）欧拉示性数（亏格）的闭式解。该解基于b元树上的传递算子、超度量结构以及无放回兄弟分割，无需蒙特卡罗模拟，与模拟结果的欧拉密度最大残差约10⁻⁴。作者将亏格标度与多重分形谱解析关联，并证明在几何权重阶梯下亏格在多重分形宽度变换下精确自相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘性级联是生成非高斯且尺度不变随机场的重要方法，其一阶统计量和多重分形谱（τ(q)、D_q、f(α)）已有精确解，但几何与拓扑度量（如欧拉示性数）的解析预测长期缺失，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 利用Greiner等人的树和累积量机制，将其特化到拓扑泛函；通过级联b元树上的传递算子结合超度量结构与守恒无放回兄弟分割，解析计算二维级联水平集的数字欧拉示性数闭式表达式。

Result: 获得了欧拉示性数（亏格）的闭式解析解，无需蒙特卡罗模拟；与模拟实现相比欧拉密度最大残差约10⁻⁴（归因于原子测度的离散化伪影）。将亏格标度与多重分形谱解析关联，证明在几何权重阶梯下亏格在多重分形宽度变换下精确自相似。守恒分割承载了微正则指纹Cov(ln w_a, ln w_b)/Var(ln w)=-1/(n-1)，区别于正则级联和对数正态级联。

Conclusion: 该成果使守恒随机排列级联成为强非高斯场形态学的受控基准模型；作为瑞利-列维飞行的乘性对应物，为预测非高斯随机场的几何与拓扑性质提供了精确的理论工具。

Abstract: The multiplicative cascade is a well-known method for generating random fields that are both genuinely non-Gaussian and scale invariant. Its one-point and scaling statistics, namely the multifractal spectra $τ(q)$, $D_q$, and $f(α)$, are known exactly; however, its geometric and topological measures are not. In this work, we show that the topological structures of a conservative (microcanonical) random permutation cascade can be precisely predicted. We compute the digital (cubical) Euler characteristic, or genus, of the level-$j$ excursion set of the two-dimensional cascade in closed form, using a transfer operator on the cascade's $b$-ary tree closed by its ultrametric structure and a conservative without-replacement sibling split. The result requires no Monte Carlo and matches simulated realizations with a maximum residual of $\sim\!10^{-4}$ in the Euler density, a controlled discretization artifact of the atomic measure. We tie the genus scaling analytically to the multifractal spectrum, and show that for a geometric ladder of weights the genus is exactly self-similar under the multifractal-width dial. The construction uses the tree-sum cumulant machinery of Greiner et al. (Phys. Rev. E 58, 554, 1998), specialized to topological functionals; the conservative split carries the microcanonical fingerprint $\mathrm{Cov}(\ln w_a,\ln w_b)/\mathrm{Var}(\ln w)=-1/(n-1)$ that distinguishes it from canonical and lognormal cascades. The cascade is the multiplicative counterpart of the Rayleigh--Lévy flight and a controlled benchmark for the morphology of strongly non-Gaussian fields.

</details>


### [123] [Free-Energy Asymptotics of the Two-Dimensional Quantum Heisenberg Ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25506)
*Andreas Klippel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维最近邻量子海森堡铁磁体在低温极限下的自由能主阶渐近行为，验证了Takahashi修正自旋波理论的预测：自由能每格点满足lim_{β→∞}β²S f₂(β,S)=-π/24。此结果填补了Napiórkowski和Seiringer留下的二维下界证明空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Napiórkowski和Seiringer证明了该公式的上界，但二维情况下的匹配下界长期未解决，Seiringer后来再次强调了这一缺口。填补这一下界证明对于完善自旋波理论的数学验证具有重要理论意义。

Method: 通过将磁振子排斥动力学与自由玻色子建立新的比较，核心技巧是将波函数扩展到碰撞构型并控制由此产生的动能代价。这种方法使得可以在二维情形下建立所需的匹配下界。

Result: 对每个固定自旋S∈{1/2,1,3/2,...}，二维最近邻量子海森堡铁磁体的自由能每格点满足lim_{β→∞}β²S f₂(β,S)=-π/24，完整证明了此前缺失的二维下界。

Conclusion: 该结果完成了对Takahashi修正自旋波理论预测的二维情形严格数学验证，填补了该领域长期存在的下界证明空白。

Abstract: We prove the leading low-temperature free-energy asymptotics of the nearest-neighbour quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet on $\mathbb Z^2$. For every fixed $S\in\{\frac12,1,\frac32,\ldots\}$, the free energy per site satisfies \[
  \lim_{β\to\infty}β^2S f_2(β,S)=-\fracπ{24}. \] This formula was predicted by Takahashi's modified spin-wave theory [15]. Napiórkowski and Seiringer proved the corresponding upper bound but left the matching lower bound in two dimensions open [13], a gap later highlighted again by Seiringer [14]. We prove this missing bound through a new comparison between the magnon exclusion dynamics and free bosons, based on extending wave functions to collision configurations with controlled kinetic cost.

</details>


### [124] [Threshold spectral transition for a fermion-boson pair on the one-dimensional lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25582)
*Sobir S. Ulashov,Shakhobiddin I. Khamidov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维格点上具有零程同位相互作用和任意质量比的费米子-玻色子二粒子 Schrödinger 算子，完整刻画了其谱性质：在非临界情形下存在唯一简单离散本征值（位于能带上下方），在临界耦合处谱边为阈值共振，并给出了弱耦合、强耦合极限下本征值与波函数的渐近行为，同时揭示了联合极限的非均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一维格点系统中费米子-玻色子二粒子对的谱结构，特别是离散本征值随耦合强度 μ、质量比 γ 和总准动量 k 的依赖关系，以及临界耦合和强耦合极限下的渐近行为，对量子多体物理和谱理论具有重要意义。

Method: 采用相对坐标变换和关于总准动量的纤维分解，将问题约化为实对称纤维算子 H_{μ,γ}(k) 在 ℓ²(Z) 上的谱分析，结合阈值共振理论和渐近展开方法。

Result: 得到能带 [2(1+γ)±2a_γ(k)]；对 μ≠0 且 a_γ(k)>0，存在唯一简单离散本征值 E_γ(k,μ)=2(1+γ)+sgn(μ)√(μ²+4a_γ(k)²)，本征函数指数衰减；临界耦合 μ=0 时谱边为阈值共振；弱耦合渐近为 |μ|²/(4a_γ(k)) 量级；强耦合时 E=μ+2(1+γ)+O(|μ|⁻¹)；分类了 (μ,γ,k)→(0,1,π) 的联合极限并揭示非均匀性。

Conclusion: 该模型在非临界情形下完全可解，其离散本征值的显式公式、临界耦合处的共振现象以及弱/强耦合渐近行为均被精确刻画，尤其是指出了联合极限的非均匀性，阐明了不同趋近路径下阈值的不同行为。

Abstract: We study a two-particle lattice Schrödinger operator describing a fermion-boson pair on the one-dimensional lattice $\mathbb Z$ with zero-range on-site interaction of strength $μ\in\mathbb R$ and mass ratio $γ>0$. Using relative coordinates and fiber decomposition with respect to the total quasi-momentum $k\in\mathbb T:=(-π,π]$, we obtain a real symmetric fiber Hamiltonian $H_{μ,γ}(k)$ in $\ell^2(\mathbb Z)$ with essential spectrum $$ [\,2(1+γ)-2a_γ(k),\,2(1+γ)+2a_γ(k)\,], \qquad a_γ(k)=\sqrt{1+2γ\cos k+γ^2}. $$
  For $a_γ(k)>0$ and $μ\ne0$, the operator has a unique simple discrete eigenvalue $$ E_γ(k,μ)=2(1+γ)+\operatorname{sgn}(μ)\sqrt{μ^2+4a_γ(k)^2}, $$ lying below the band for $μ<0$ and above it for $μ>0$, with an exponentially decaying eigenfunction. At the exceptional fiber $(γ,k)=(1,π)$, the band collapses to ${4}$ and the unique simple eigenvalue is $E_1(π,μ)=4+μ$.
  At the critical coupling $μ=0$ and $a_γ(k)>0$, both spectral edges are threshold resonances with bounded non-square-integrable solutions. As $μ\to0^\pm$, the discrete eigenvalue approaches the corresponding threshold with $$ |E_γ(k,μ)-E_{\mathrm{thr}}^\pm(k)| =\frac{μ^2}{4a_γ(k)}+O(μ^4), $$ and the normalized eigenfunction converges to the resonant solution. For strong coupling, $E_γ(k,μ)=μ+2(1+γ)+O(|μ|^{-1})$, while the eigenfunction localizes at the interaction site. We also classify the joint limit $μ\to0^\pm$, $(γ,k)\to(1,π)$ according to the relative scale of $|μ|$ and $a_γ(k)$, revealing the nonuniformity of the weak-coupling threshold asymptotics.

</details>


### [125] [Lower bounds on the spatial decay of the ground state of the Pauli-Fierz model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25719)
*Fumio Hiroshima,Yuki Tsujimoto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非相对论量子电动力学中Pauli-Fierz模型基态的点态空间衰减下界。对于完整模型，通过基于Feynman-Kac公式的概率方法推导下界；对于偶极近似模型，由于被积函数变为确定性，可借助Agmon距离方法获得下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Pauli-Fierz模型基态的空间衰减下界，因为完整模型中应用Agmon距离方法面临双重随机积分的路径依赖这一严重困难，需要寻找替代方法（概率方法），并与偶极近似模型下的结果进行对比。

Method: 对于完整Pauli-Fierz模型，采用基于Feynman-Kac公式的概率方法推导基态点态空间衰减的下界；对于偶极近似模型，由于被积函数变为确定性，可以使用Agmon距离方法得到下界。

Result: 完整模型下通过概率方法成功获得基态空间衰减下界；偶极近似模型下基于Agmon距离获得相应的下界。两种模型均实现了对基态空间衰减下界的刻画。

Conclusion: 针对完整Pauli-Fierz模型的基态空间衰减下界，概率方法（Feynman-Kac）是克服Agmon距离方法中双重随机积分路径依赖困难的有效替代方案；在偶极近似下，Agmon距离方法则可以成功应用。

Abstract: Lower bounds on the pointwise spatial decay of the ground state of the Pauli-Fierz model in non-relativistic quantum electrodynamics are studied.Two models are considered: the full Pauli-Fierz model and the Pauli-Fierz model under the dipole approximation. For the full model, a lower bound is derived by means of a probabilistic approach based on the Feynman-Kac formula. The application of the Agmon distance method to the full model encounters a serious difficulty arising from the path dependence of a double stochastic integral. In contrast, under the dipole approximation, the corresponding integrand becomes deterministic, which allows a lower bound to be obtained in terms of the Agmon distance.

</details>


### [126] [Plasticity as Directional Stationarity: Yielding, Flow, and Hardening from One Functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25991)
*Huilong Ren*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的塑性理论统一框架，通过单一标量泛函对单向容许塑性路径的方向平稳性来组织传统塑性力学的所有核心关系（弹性律、屈服条件、流动法则、硬化关系和加卸载条件）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统塑性理论由弹性律、屈服条件、流动法则、硬化关系和加卸载条件等多个独立构件组合而成，各构件之间缺乏统一的形式化基础。本文旨在通过一个标量泛函的方向平稳性原理将这些分散的构件统一到一个数学框架中，从而更清晰地揭示塑性响应的内在结构及其关联关系。

Method: 该方法以单个标量泛函在单向容许塑性路径上的方向平稳性为核心：其一阶变分确定应力、内变量力和活性阻力；将其限制在容许塑性切线上定义各机制约化方向力，其符号与单向平稳性给出弹性不等式、互补关系、加卸载条件和活性一致性条件。通过切线是否与屈服边界法线对齐区分关联与非关联流动；自相似性分析识别正齐次应力度量作为广泛关联族，并分离屈服面形状、各向同性扩张与运动平移的作用；储存与阻力项涵盖各向同性、运动、耦合和梯度硬化，多个活性变量描述独立激活机制；粘性势将构造推广至率相关演化。

Result: 论文给出了四个闭式解，分别展示了多活性区域、运动硬化场、压力敏感极限状态，以及法向与非法向流动对应的不同位移场。附录还提供了时间离散的本构积分和量纲检验。

Conclusion: 该统一框架通过方向平稳性原理有效地整合了塑性力学的各核心构件，能够统一描述关联/非关联流动、多种硬化类型和率相关行为，并以闭式解验证了框架的适用范围和有效性。

Abstract: Traditional plasticity theory is commonly organized through an elastic law, a yield condition, a flow rule, hardening relations, and loading--unloading conditions. This paper formulates these relations through directional stationarity of one scalar functional evaluated over one-sided admissible plastic paths. The first variation determines stress, internal-variable forces, and activity resistance. Restriction to an admissible plastic tangent defines a reduced directional force for each mechanism; its sign and one-sided stationarity give the elastic inequality, complementarity, loading--unloading conditions, and active consistency. Associated response corresponds to alignment between the admissible tangent and the normal to the resulting yield boundary, whereas a nonparallel tangent represents non-associated flow. A self-similarity analysis identifies positively homogeneous stress gauges as a broad associated family and separates the roles of yield-surface shape, isotropic expansion, and kinematic translation. The storage and resistance terms cover isotropic, kinematic, coupled, and gradient hardening, while multiple activity variables describe independently activated mechanisms. A viscous potential extends the construction to rate-dependent evolution. Four closed-form solutions illustrate multi-activity regions, kinematic-hardening fields, pressure-sensitive limit states, and the distinct displacement fields associated with normal and non-normal flow. Time-discrete constitutive integration and dimensional checks are collected in the appendices.

</details>
